# ema-lufthansa, standing order 50 (2026-11-19): full-site no-breakage sweep of Brussels, ITA and Discover. Every served
# document of each site (the DA upload list: every language and market root; the nav and footer documents are rendered
# inside every page, not as pages of their own) on brussels / ita / discover.turnsthe.page at 1440 x 900 and 390 x 844, one
# page at a time in one browser per host (full Chrome, Liberation Sans for Arial, airline hosts refused), each load
# waited to network idle, scrolled to the end for the lazy pictures, waited again and every section loaded before
# measuring. Faults looked for: broken picture, sideways scroll, a section or block that never loads, an element out of
# the page or its column, block-level siblings drawn over each other, an empty section or column, a heading in another
# size than its same-level siblings, a list in another size than its paragraphs, a page more than 15 % shorter or longer
# than its source-language copy at the same width (Brussels be/en, ITA it/en, Discover de/de).
# Classes: fixed:<commit> (a rule for a kind on 3 or more documents), listed (1 or 2 documents, or not a layout fault:
# transient, function), content (www's page or the document's own text, pictures or blocks), by design (boxes that cross
# where nothing drawn crosses).
